  get monthlyHistory(){let labels=['Janeiro','Fevereiro','Março','Abril','Maio','Junho','Julho','Agosto','Setembro','Outubro','Novembro','Dezembro'];return labels.map((label,i)=>{let key=String(this.dashboardYear)+'-'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),payments=this.yearlyPaymentRows.filter(r=>String(r.paymentDate||'').slice(0,7)===key),services=this.yearlyRows.filter(s=>String(s.serviceDate||'').slice(0,7)===key),paid=payments.reduce((a,r)=>a+Number(r.total||0),0),open=services.reduce((a,s)=>a+this.balance(s),0),map={};payments.forEach(r=>{map[r.category]=(map[r.category]||0)+Number(r.total||0)});let top=Object.entries(map).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];return{key,label,count:payments.length,paid,open,topCategory:top?top[0]:'-'}})},
  get annualTopProviders(){let map={};this.yearlyPaymentRows.forEach(r=>{let id=r.providerId,p=this.providers.find(x=>Number(x.id)===Number(id))||{};if(!map[id])map[id]={id,name:p.name||'-',category:p.category||r.category,count:0,total:0,open:0};map[id].count++;map[id].total+=Number(r.total||0)});this.yearlyRows.forEach(s=>{let id=s.providerId,p=this.providers.find(x=>Number(x.id)===Number(id))||{};if(!map[id])map[id]={id,name:p.name||'-',category:p.category||s.category,count:0,total:0,open:0};map[id].open+=this.balance(s)});return Object.values(map).filter(x=>x.total>0||x.open>0).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total).slice(0,10)},

      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;
  },

launchMonths=this.reportRows.map(s=>String(s.serviceDate||'').slice(0,7)).filter(Boolean),paidMonths=this.reportDetailRows.filter(s=>Number(s.reportValue||0)>0).map(s=>String(s.paymentDate||'').slice(0,7)).filter(Boolean),months=[...new Set([...launchMonths,...paidMonths])].sort(), er=document.getElementById('repChart');if(er){if(this.charts.rep){this.charts.rep.destroy();this.charts.rep=null}er.removeAttribute('height');er.removeAttribute('width');er.style.height='240px';er.style.maxHeight='240px';er.style.width='100%';this.charts.rep=new Chart(er,{type:'line',data:{labels:months.map(m=>this.month(m)),datasets:[{label:'Lançado',data:months.map(m=>this.reportRows.filter(s=>String(s.serviceDate||'').slice(0,7)===m).reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.amount||0),0)),tension:.35},{label:'Pago',data:months.map(m=>this.reportDetailRows.filter(s=>String(s.paymentDate||'').slice(0,7)===m).reduce((a,s)=>a+Number(s.reportValue||0),0)),tension:.35}]},options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,resizeDelay:150,animation:false,plugins:{legend:{position:'bottom'}},scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true}}}})}}
}}
</script>

<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-qrcode-force-visible">
(function(){
function buildQr(){
  const codeEl=document.getElementById('receiptAuthCodeVisible');
  const qrEl=document.getElementById('receiptQrCodeVisible');

  if(!codeEl || !qrEl) return;

  const now=new Date();
  const code='RC-'+now.getFullYear()+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+
  String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*90000+10000);

  codeEl.textContent=code;

  qrEl.innerHTML='';

  if(typeof QRCode!=='undefined'){
      new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code,
      width:94,
      height:94
    });
  }else{
    qrEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;color:#0F2A3A">QR indisponível</div>';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('load',buildQr);
setTimeout(buildQr,1200);
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-report-logo-sync">
window.addEventListener('load',function(){
  try{
    const existingLogo=document.querySelector('.rc-icon,.receipt-logo img,.app-logo img,img');
    const reportLogo=document.getElementById('rcReportLogo');

    if(existingLogo && reportLogo && existingLogo.src){
      reportLogo.src=existingLogo.src;
    }
  }catch(e){}
});
</script>

  get filteredProviders(){
    let q=(this.q||'').toLowerCase();
    let ps=(this.providerSearch||'').toLowerCase();
    let cat=this.providerCategoryFilter||'';
    let list=this.providers.filter(p=>{
      if(q&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
      if(ps&&![p.name,p.document,p.category,p.phone,p.whatsapp].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(ps))return false;
      if(cat&&p.category!==cat)return false;
      return true;
    });
    let field=this.providerSortField||'name';
    let dir=this.providerSortDir||'asc';
    list=[...list].sort((a,b)=>{
      let va=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(a.id):String(a[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      let vb=field==='spend'?this.providerSpend(b.id):String(b[field]||'').toLowerCase();
      if(va<vb)return dir==='asc'?-1:1;
      if(va>vb)return dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      return 0;
    });
    return list;

<script id="rc-qrcode-script">
(function(){
  function createReceiptAuth(){
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('receiptAuthCode');
    const qrEl = document.getElementById('receiptQrCode');
    if(!codeEl || !qrEl || typeof QRCode === 'undefined') return;

    const stamp = new Date();
    const code = 'RC-' +
      stamp.getFullYear() + '-' +
      String(stamp.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') +
      String(stamp.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + '-' +
      Math.floor(Math.random()*9000+1000);

    codeEl.textContent = code;

    qrEl.innerHTML = '';

    new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code + '|Emitido em ' + stamp.toLocaleString('pt-BR'),
      width:76,
      height:76
    });
  }

  if(document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', createReceiptAuth);
  }else{
    createReceiptAuth();
  }
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-qrcode-force-visible">
(function(){
function buildQr(){
  const codeEl=document.getElementById('receiptAuthCodeVisible');
  const qrEl=document.getElementById('receiptQrCodeVisible');

  if(!codeEl || !qrEl) return;

  const now=new Date();
  const code='RC-'+now.getFullYear()+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+
  String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*90000+10000);

  codeEl.textContent=code;

  qrEl.innerHTML='';

  if(typeof QRCode!=='undefined'){
      new QRCode(qrEl,{
      text:'ReciboCondo|' + code,
      width:94,
      height:94
    });
  }else{
    qrEl.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:center;color:#0F2A3A">QR indisponível</div>';
  }
}
window.addEventListener('load',buildQr);
setTimeout(buildQr,1200);
})();
</script>


<script id="rc-report-logo-sync">
window.addEventListener('load',function(){
  try{
    const existingLogo=document.querySelector('.rc-icon,.receipt-logo img,.app-logo img,img');
    const reportLogo=document.getElementById('rcReportLogo');

    if(existingLogo && reportLogo && existingLogo.src){
      reportLogo.src=existingLogo.src;
    }
  }catch(e){}
});
</script>
